# 09 — EN–ID Translation Review

Human-readable view of `08-en-id-translation-dataset.csv`, grouped by scope and module. **REVIEW** rows show the proposed text in *italics* (recommended option, not final). **STRUCTURAL_PREP_REQUIRED** rows have a proposed translation that is linguistically ready but not implementation-ready (see 10). DO_NOT_TRANSLATE rows keep the source text.
